Out of 2,656 players in the Legacy Grand Prix here in Las Vegas, only ten emerge from the first day's trenches with a perfect 9-0. Lots of Grixis Delver and Neo-Miracles abound. But the Infect deck that's still ravaging the tournament halls has gotten the most notice.

#### Mathew Chung's White-Blue Control – GP Vegas, 9-0
